Build helper:
@echo off
setlocal
cd /d "%~dp0.."
call "C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft Visual Studio\2022\BuildTools\VC\Auxiliary\Build\vcvars64.bat" >nul
if errorlevel 1 exit /b 1
set "CUDA_PATH=%CD%\build-local\cuda-13.0"
set "PATH=%CUDA_PATH%\bin;%CUDA_PATH%\bin\x64;%PATH%"
cmake -S backends/glm5next -B build-local/glm5next-candidate-cuda -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release "-DSTRATA_GLM_ARCHIVE=%CD%/build-local/llama-glm-86ebfef2.tar.gz" -DSTRATA_GLM_ARCHIVE_SHA256=f8e524b635b726bae74fd8f84bb9249e5b09384c63207f6707c5a3f921acad99 -DSTRATA_GLM_CUDA=ON -DSTRATA_GLM_GRAPH_CHECK=ON -DSTRATA_GLM_SYNC_BASELINE=ON -DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=120 "-DCMAKE_CUDA_COMPILER=%CUDA_PATH%/bin/nvcc.exe" "-DCUDAToolkit_ROOT=%CUDA_PATH%"
if errorlevel 1 exit /b 1
cmake --build build-local/glm5next-candidate-cuda --config Release -j 4
if errorlevel 1 exit /b 1
ctest --test-dir build-local/glm5next-candidate-cuda -C Release -V --no-tests=error
exit /b %errorlevel%
